{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"grafts","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"grafting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"grafted","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"grafted","tags":["past"]},{"form":"graff","tags":["alternative","archaic"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To insert (a graft) in a branch or stem of another tree; to propagate by insertion in another stock; also, to insert a graft upon."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To insert scions (grafts) from one tree, or kind of tree, etc., into another; to practice grafting."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To implant a portion of (living flesh or akin) in a lesion so as to form an organic union."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["medicine","sciences","surgery"]},{"glosses":["To join (one thing) to another as if by grafting, so as to bring about a close union."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To cover, as a ring bolt, block strap, splicing, etc., with a weaving of small cord or rope yarns."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["To form a graft polymer"],"topics":["chemistry","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɡɹɑːft/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɡɹæft/","tags":["Northern-England","US"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"to join as if by grafting","word":"affix"},{"word":"attach"},{"word":"put together"},{"word":"unite"},{"word":"imp"}],"upstream_line":24752,"upstream_offset":169183300,"upstream_record_sha256":"bcf1ceef4889ed9931bc487da476b4f88390b9bae210e158626af2f416447bac","word":"graft"}
